HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1588adad367d99183622","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:4:2\",\"root_000434\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bu yapı, insanı amaçlı çalışma ve bu çalışmanın karşılığını alma düzenine elverişli kılan bir yetkinliktir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B001","root_000434/B001","root_000434/B003","root_001273/B008","root_001273/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3c0e0262a034182201c6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:4:2\",\"root_000434\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]],[\"95:4:5\",\"root_000323\",[\"B001\"]],[\"95:4:6\",\"root_001273\",[\"B008\",\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yaratılış, yalnızca bir beden kurmak değil, insanı ölçülü, dengeli ve dimdik bir standarda göre biçimlendirmektir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000555/B001","root_001273/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_48ea69ed1035244a4b84","text":"5. **An uprightness that can culminate in standing again.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ق و م/B013` supplies resurrection, the rising of the Hour, and people standing before their Lord. It is activated by later `ر د د/B001` return (95:5), `د ي ن/B002` reckoning (95:7), and the divine judge of 95:8. **Structural cue:** initial *taqwīm* is followed by descent, an exception with uncut reward, then reckoning and judgment. **Assigned role:** the resurrection branch makes upright standing the possible completion of the trajectory rather than only its starting posture. **Abductive move:** the packet does not explicitly state a second rising; I infer it from the accepted standing-before-the-Lord branch combined with return and adjudication. **Reading change:** the ayah’s upright formation acquires an eschatological horizon, so the downward turn need not be the human’s final state.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_000059/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5322e304fd348b317c4c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:4:3\",\"root_000059\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İnsan, çevredeki yabani ve insansız alanın karşısında görünür, tanıdık ve yerleşik bir varlığın ortaya çıkışı olarak da belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B004","root_001273/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_552ab1e69fc474d22ec4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:4:6\",\"root_001273\",[\"B004\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dimdik yapı, korunması ve yönetilmesi gereken bir doğruluk düzeni kurduğu için insanı sonraki hesap ve hüküm karşısında sorumlu bir konuma yerleştirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B003","root_000434/B004","root_001273/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_57ba04eafb2442f577a2","text":"2. **A relational and civic capacity.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ء ن س/B003` supplies familiarity and companionship that remove estrangement; fixed `خ ل ق/B004` supplies inner disposition and moral bearing; fixed `ق و م/B004` supplies care, guardianship, maintenance, and governance. **Structural cue:** the created human is bracketed by the trustworthy city (95:3) and, later, trusting assent, sound deeds, and judgment among people (95:6–8). **Assigned role:** *insān* names relational presence, *khalq* an inward social disposition, and *taqwīm* the capacity to maintain an ordered common life. **Abductive move:** the packet does not define “best setting” as civic competence; I infer it because the human root’s anti-estrangement branch and the *q-w-m* maintenance branch become functional inside the surrounding trust-and-judgment sequence. **Reading change:** best formation includes the ability to sustain trustworthy relations and institutions, not bodily shape alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_000059/B003","root_000955/B006","root_000955/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7279f89df281c0ea3ec9","text":"2. **Human presence as domestication against wildness and absence.** Lexical evidence: ء ن س B001, visible human presence against hidden/wild beings; ء ن س B003, familiarity removing estrangement; ط و ر B006 in 95:2, wildness beyond the domestic limit; ط و ر B007, no one present in a place. Structural cue: the word الإنسان stands after topographical signs and before a fall into lowliness. Assigned role: insan supplies the presence/familiarity pole, while ط و ر supplies the possible wild/empty opposite. Abductive move: I infer that \"human\" is not just species but the emergence of visible, social, familiar presence within a bounded world. Reading change: creation in best taqwim becomes the taming/presencing of a creature capable of social familiarity, not only anatomical excellence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B001","root_000434/B001","root_000434/B003","root_001273/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7e8381424ef7ba49adff","text":"1. **Creation as measured proportion before any moral verdict.** Lexical evidence: خ ل ق B001, measuring/proportioning; خ ل ق B003, complete well-proportioned form; ح س ن B001, goodness/beauty opposed to ugliness; ق و م B008/B011, straightness and bodily stature. Structural cue: the ayah combines created human, best, and taqwim in a dense cluster of making, beauty, and uprightness. Assigned role: خلق supplies the act of measured making; حسن qualifies the quality; تقويم supplies upright measured stance. Abductive move: I infer that the oath sequence has been gathering witnesses to a standard by which the human's form can be called \"best.\" Reading change: the ayah reads as a claim of calibrated proportion, not merely biological creation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B002","root_001273/B002","root_001273/B004","root_001273/B008","root_001273/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_892dcc01c0c1c92cfd92","text":"3. **Taqwim as uprightness, governance, and eventual court.** Lexical evidence: ق و م B002, bodily standing; ق و م B004, care/guardianship/governing; ق و م B008, straightness/uprightness; ق و م B013, resurrection/standing; ح ك م B002 in 95:8, judgment. Structural cue: تقويم is followed by الرد downward, then exception, then judgment. Assigned role: ق و م makes the human not only upright in body but placed under a regime of maintenance, straightness, and later standing-before-judgment. Abductive move: I supply the arrow that a formed uprightness implies accountability when later ayat ask about الدين and الحكم. Reading change: \"best taqwim\" becomes a legally and eschatologically loaded posture, not merely physical symmetry.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B007","root_001290/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8b1e47189fc5ad741af3","text":"5. **A shadow branch: fabricated speech threatens the created order.** Lexical evidence: خ ل ق B007, fabricating false speech; ك ذ ب B001/B002 in 95:7, falsehood and declaring false. Structural cue: after the creation claim and fall/exception, 95:7 asks what makes one deny. Assigned role: the same root خلق can move from divine proportioning to human fabrication when activated by يكذبك. Abductive move: I infer a contrast between God's measured making and the denier's invented counter-speech. Reading change: 95:4 becomes a standard against which denial looks like counterfeit making.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000015/B001","root_000434/B005","root_001046/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8ef4b8870c979fcac2d8","text":"4. **Aptness for work and reward.** Lexical evidence: خ ل ق B005, worthiness/aptness; ع م ل B001/B008 in 95:6, intentional work and fitness for work; ء ج ر B001, recompense for work. Structural cue: the exception in 95:6 is phrased as believing and doing صالحات, followed by أجر. Assigned role: creation makes the human \"fit\" for the work/reward circuit that later distinguishes the exception. Abductive move: I infer that the created form is a capacity for accountable action, activated by the later عمل/أجر pair. Reading change: the ayah reads as enabling condition for responsible work, not only praise of original form.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B002","root_001273/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-8","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8f9a923d5f9984d9b22f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:4:6\",\"root_001273\",[\"B002\",\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Dimdik kurulma, aşağı dönüşün ardından hesap için yeniden ayağa kalkma ihtimalini de taşır; ilk duruş böylece son duruşa açılır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_928d5c642f10d0648d9e","text":"95:5 first overturned a static reading of “best setting”: upright proportion became a reversible state on a vertical axis. The larger surprise came from 95:6–8, where work, recompense, reckoning, and judgment activated fixed `ق و م/B010`, `ق و م/B015`, and `ق و م/B013`; *taqwīm* retrospectively carried appraisal and possible standing-before-the-Lord, not anatomy alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002","root_001290/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_94be6cfbe5dc7237caab","text":"3. **Perceptual equipment that makes denial accountable.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ء ن س/B002` supplies perceiving or detecting by sight, hearing, or inward awareness. Later `ك ذ ب/B002` supplies declaring a report or person false (95:7), and `ح ك م/B003` supplies knowledge that reaches the truth (95:8). **Structural cue:** visible landscape markers fill 95:1–3; after the claim of human formation, 95:7 asks how falsification remains possible. **Assigned role:** the *insān* branch supplies the perceptual faculty by which the preceding signs can enter awareness. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies objects, perception, and the later challenge but does not say perception causes assent; I infer only that equipped awareness raises the evidentiary cost of denial. **Reading change:** “the human” is formed as a discerner, so *aḥsani taqwīm* has an epistemic dimension that grounds the later question.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B003","root_000434/B004","root_001273/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ab6e769360a3707b3801","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:4:2\",\"root_000434\",[\"B004\"]],[\"95:4:3\",\"root_000059\",[\"B003\"]],[\"95:4:6\",\"root_001273\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İnsan adı, yabancılığı gideren tanışıklık ve birliktelik anlamıyla, en iyi yapının toplumsal ilişki kurma ve ortak düzeni sürdürme yeteneğini de içerdiğini gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001","root_001273/B010","root_001273/B015"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b8aef536feb5c093fb91","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:4:2\",\"root_000434\",[\"B001\"]],[\"95:4:6\",\"root_001273\",[\"B010\",\"B015\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"En iyi yapı, insana verilmiş değeri ve daha sonra davranışlarının tartılacağı kesin ölçüyü başlangıçtan kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B001","root_000434/B001","root_001273/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d280fe4780123d92eab7","text":"1. **Calibrated, upright formation.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `خ ل ق/B001` supplies measuring and proportioning before an act, and `خ ل ق/B003` complete, balanced visible form; fixed `ح س ن/B001` supplies goodness or beauty against ugliness; fixed `ق و م/B008` supplies straightness and evenness, and `ق و م/B011` bodily stature and proportion. **Structural cue:** the construction places the human “in” the superlative *aḥsan* of *taqwīm*, immediately before the antithetical double low of 95:5. **Assigned role:** creation supplies measured production, *aḥsan* its optimal quality, and *taqwīm* its straight bodily and normative axis. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies compatible branches but no engineering metaphor; I infer that the phrase describes formation against a calibrated standard. **Reading change:** the ayah becomes a claim of deliberately proportioned uprightness, not generic creation followed by a free-standing compliment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d368ced8df529246b3d2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:4:3\",\"root_000059\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İnsan adı aynı zamanda görme, işitme ve içten sezme gücünü öne çıkarır; bu donanım, sonraki inkârı delil eksikliğinden çok sorumluluk sorunu hâline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-9","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_dfa5f041f7bc52b4f9f2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:4:2\",\"root_000434\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yaratmanın uydurma söz anlamı, ilahi ölçülü yaratılışa karşı inkârı insanın ürettiği sahte bir karşı-yaratım gibi gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e99093e328dd1d6775b8","text":"95:8's أحكم was the biggest retrospective activator for 95:4. It echoes أحسن and makes \"best taqwim\" a measured standard that requires a best judge, not merely an admired form. 95:6 also made aptness-for-work unavoidable: خلق B005, عمل, صالحات, and أجر form a capacity-action-recompense chain. The surprising correction is that 95:4 is not only anthropology; it is the setup for a legal/accountable posture.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000015/B001","root_000434/B001","root_001273/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f1ea581a6a5e77cea5fc","text":"4. **Formation as valuation before reckoning.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ق و م/B010` supplies value and appraisal, while `ق و م/B015` supplies exact, even weight; fixed `خ ل ق/B001` already supplies measurement. Later `ء ج ر/B001` names recompense for work, `د ي ن/B002` reckoning and recompense, and `ح ك م/B002` adjudication. **Structural cue:** measured creation precedes differentiated deeds, reward, reckoning, and a final comparison among judges. **Assigned role:** *taqwīm* is the initial appraisal or establishment of worth; later recompense tests action against that endowed measure. **Abductive move:** the packet gives the valuation vocabulary but no ledger; I infer a benchmark mechanism in which measured capacity makes later evaluation intelligible. **Reading change:** “best *taqwīm*” can denote conferred value and evaluability as well as straightening.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
